        Idea:
Produce a design document that plans the full migration of reigh-worker's vendored deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP code from its current drifted snapshot (~50 days / ~9 minor versions behind, no .git directory) into a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, broken into 2-week sprints. The document's job is to define the sprints, not to execute them.

Non-negotiable constraint throughout every sprint: NO FUNCTIONALITY MAY BE LOST. Every behavior reigh-worker currently depends on — IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose/depth/canny/cameraman modes (source/core/params/travel_guidance.py _IC_LORA_BY_MODE, _auto_inject_travel_guidance_lora), the newly-landed ltx_anchor travel guidance kind, the existing ltx_control and ltx_hybrid travel paths, the vendored ICLoraPipeline behavior, the custom patches our vendored tree carries — must survive, be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan, and have a verification step.

Context the document must incorporate (treat as authoritative background):

- Current situation: reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ is a flat copy without a .git directory. Cannot be rebased. Upstream is deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP. Approximately 50 days behind (vendored top changelog entry is v10.01 / 2026-01-01; upstream top is v10.951 / 2026-02-19). 22 files differ in Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ alone, ~4303 diff lines.
- Missing upstream features we want: ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json defaults, shared/utils/self_refiner.py (Feb 4), models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py (res_2s sampler), outpaint IC-LoRA + gamma preprocessing (_apply_gamma_to_media, LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA, LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO constants), prompt_enhancer improvements.
- Upstream's IC-LoRA pattern is simpler than ours: single union-control preload URL for distilled 22B (models/ltx2/ltx2.py:783-832), substring-matched, no per-mode Python dict like our _IC_LORA_BY_MODE. Upstream auto-forces guide_phases=1 when P/D/E in video_prompt_type (ltx2.py:918). Cameraman-style per-mode LoRAs are 19B (not 22B). This means a large chunk of our custom IC-LoRA registry logic is either convergeable to upstream's approach or should become a thin override.
- Detailer situation: LTX-2 detailer is 19B only (no 22B/2.3 variant exists). Lightricks' ICLoraPipeline runs LoRA stage-1-only with stage-2 latent chaining. Upstream has no LTX-2 detailer integration. self_refiner (PnP mid-denoise) is the nearest thing upstream offers and is likely to obviate inline detailing after we adopt it.
- User preference: detailer, if we build it, should be a post-gen task handler (like source/task_handlers/rife_interpolate.py) rather than inline in the travel pipeline. This is independent of the fork migration but is a natural follow-up sprint.
- Constraint inventory (what reigh-worker currently depends on that must survive):
  - IC-LoRA auto-injection by mode (source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987) wiring union-control to pose/depth/canny and cameraman LoRA to cameraman mode.
  - Pixel cross-fade guide video path for ltx_control (source/media/video/travel_guide.py) — must NOT be broken by the resync even though it's arguably legacy.
  - ltx_anchor kind just shipped in commit 9656cc90 — must survive the resync. The rename _build_segment_hybrid_guidance_config → _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config touched vendored tests indirectly; verify.
  - The vendored Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79 clear_conditioning that strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode — load-bearing for ltx_anchor, must not be accidentally reverted to an upstream version lacking it if we have patches here.
  - IC-LoRA dedup logic (task_registry.py:961-968).
  - Travel orchestrator PATH A skip for ltx_anchor (orchestrator.py:1278-1289) and _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config rename (orchestrator.py).
  - Existing test suite: tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py (20 ltx_anchor tests), tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py (8 runtime tests incl. source tripwire), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py (23 tests, recently promoted to tracked via .gitignore allowlist), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py, tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py, tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py.

Open design decisions the document must resolve (as Settled Decisions with rationale):

1. Fork shape: submodule pinned to upstream SHA vs. our own long-running fork on GitHub (e.g. banodoco/Wan2GP) vs. monkeypatch-only with upstream as a dep. Probably a hybrid — fork + minimal monkeypatch layer for things that shouldn't be in the fork. Document must pick one concretely.
2. Patch triage buckets: every deviation between our vendored tree and upstream gets classified as (a) accept upstream — stale not intentional, (b) upstreamable — PR to deepbeepmeep, (c) banodoco-specific — carry in fork or as override, (d) cruft — drop. Document must define the triage process as a sprint task.
3. Initial resync cadence: do we cut over to upstream head all at once, or stage by subsystem (e.g., models/ltx2/ first, then shared/, then postprocessing/)? Document must pick.
4. LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion: bundle into the migration, or hold for a separate A/B sprint? Document must pick.
5. res_2s / self_refiner exposure: off-by-default flags, or promote to default once upstream does? Document must pick.
6. Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion: does this sit inside the fork migration sprint plan (as a sprint 3 or 4), or is it a separate initiative? Document must pick.
7. Rebase cadence post-migration: weekly? Monthly? Triggered by specific upstream events? Document must pick.
8. CI / drift detection: how do we avoid re-drifting once we're synced? Document must propose a specific mechanism (scheduled CI job comparing to upstream head, etc.).

Target shape of the final document:
- Executive summary (what, why, how long, what we're protecting)
- Current-state inventory (what's vendored, what's drifted, what's load-bearing)
- Fork architecture decision (the Settled Decisions on shape, patch triage, cadence)
- Sprint breakdown: 3-5 sprints of 2 weeks each, each with: goals, deliverables, verification gates, rollback plan, explicit functionality-preservation checks. First sprint should be inventory-heavy (the diff triage is where the risk lives). Last sprint should cover sustainability (rebase cadence, CI, detection).
- Risk register: what could go wrong per sprint, probability, impact, mitigation
- Success criteria: objective checks for migration complete vs migration in progress vs regression observed
- Explicit Settled Decisions section using the canonical megaplan format (id: SD-NNN, load_bearing, decision, rationale)

Out of scope (document should note these explicitly as non-goals): flipping ltx_anchor to default, real-GPU empirical validation of the new defaults, upscaler-pass anchor re-injection, identity-drift fixes.

Honor the memory-note constraint: do NOT include hard-halt prerequisite clauses of the form halt when MX not shipped. Each sprint must be able to proceed, revise scope, or explicitly deprioritize — but not sit in force-proceed limbo.

````md
**Scope:** Medium

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

        # Implementation Plan: Wan2GP Fork Migration Design Document

## Overview
Produce a design document at `reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` that plans (does **not** execute) the migration of reigh-worker's vendored, `.git`-less Wan2GP snapshot (v10.01, ~50 days / ~9 minor versions behind upstream v10.951) into a fork-plus-patch-layer relationship. The document is organized as 3-5 two-week sprints and must resolve 8 specified open design decisions as Settled Decisions (SD-NNN).

Non-negotiable: **no functionality loss**. Every reigh-worker behavior listed in the brief's constraint inventory (IC-LoRA auto-injection, `ltx_anchor`/`ltx_hybrid`/`ltx_control` travel kinds, `clear_conditioning`, pixel cross-fade guide, test suite) must be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan with a verification step.

Confirmed repo state (from pre-plan inspection):
- `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` exists as flat tree, `.git` absent, top CHANGELOG entry `v10.01 / January 1st 2026` (matches brief).
- `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` at `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:32`; `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` at :31.
- IC-LoRA auto-injection + dedup at `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987` (brief's line range is exact).
- Travel orchestrator lives at `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py` (brief said `orchestrator.py`; corrected path). PATH A skip at :1272-1289. `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config` at :178, called at :2126. `is_ltx_anchor` branches at :134, :231, :2115.
- `clear_conditioning` at `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66`.
- All mentioned test files exist in `reigh-worker/tests/`.

Scope of this task: **write the design document**. Out of scope: any code change, any execution of migration sprints, any upstream PR drafting.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Confirm constraint-inventory anchors (`reigh-worker/source/`, `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`, `reigh-worker/tests/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Verify** each touch point named in the brief resolves to a current file+line before citing it in the document. Already confirmed in pre-plan inspection — reuse those line numbers verbatim:
   - `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:31-37` (IC-LoRA registry).
   - `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987` (auto-inject + dedup).
   - `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py:178,231,1272-1289,2115,2126` (anchor rename + PATH A skip).
   - `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79` (clear_conditioning).

   - `tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py`, `tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py`, `tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py`, `tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py`, `tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py`, `tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py`.

### Step 2: Draft the design document skeleton (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** the file using this structure (order is load-bearing; reviewers scan top-down):
   ```
   # Wan2GP Fork Migration — Design Document
   ## 1. Executive Summary       (what/why/how long/what's protected)
   ## 2. Current-State Inventory (vendored tree, drift, load-bearing code)
   ## 3. Fork Architecture Decisions (the 8 Settled Decisions)

   ## 7. Success Criteria
   ## 8. Non-Goals

   ```
2. **Populate Section 1** (Executive Summary, ≤15 lines): one-sentence problem statement; sprint count + cadence; "what's protected" bullet list citing the constraint inventory in one glance.
3. **Populate Section 2** (Current-State Inventory): tabulate vendored version (v10.01), upstream head (v10.951), delta (~50 days / ~9 minor / 22 files in `models/ltx2/` / ~4303 diff lines), missing upstream features (ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json, self_refiner.py, res2s.py, outpaint IC-LoRA + gamma, prompt_enhancer), and banodoco-carry items (IC-LoRA per-mode registry, ltx_anchor kind, clear_conditioning strip, PATH A skip, dedup).
4. **Populate Section 8** (Non-Goals) verbatim from brief: no flipping `ltx_anchor` to default; no real-GPU empirical validation of new defaults; no upscaler-pass anchor re-injection; no identity-drift fixes.

### Step 3: Resolve the 8 open design decisions (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §3, §9)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Write** Section 9 in canonical megaplan format. Each entry:
   ```
   ### SD-001
   - load_bearing: true|false
   - decision: <one-sentence resolution>
   - rationale: <why, 2-4 sentences referencing constraints or memory>
   ```
2. **Resolve** the 8 decisions from the brief. Recommended resolutions (executor may revise with concrete counter-evidence):
   - **SD-001 Fork shape** (load_bearing: true): Hybrid — `banodoco/Wan2GP` long-running fork on GitHub, consumed as git submodule at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`, plus a thin `reigh-worker/source/runtime/wan2gp_patches/` Python monkeypatch layer for items that shouldn't live in the fork (env-specific shims, patches upstream would reject). Rationale: pure submodule loses monkeypatch agility for narrow overrides; pure monkeypatch can't carry file-level forks like `ltx_core/tools.py:clear_conditioning`; hybrid keeps fork small and monkeypatch small.
   - **SD-002 Patch triage buckets** (load_bearing: true): Four buckets per diff hunk — (a) accept-upstream, (b) upstreamable (file PR to deepbeepmeep), (c) banodoco-fork (carry in fork), (d) cruft (drop). Triage is Sprint 1's primary deliverable; each hunk lands in a CSV at `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` with columns `path,hunk,bucket,owner,verification`.
   - **SD-003 Resync cadence** (load_bearing: true): Stage by subsystem, not all-at-once. Order: `shared/` + `postprocessing/` (low-risk utilities including self_refiner) → `models/ltx2/` (the 22-file hot zone) → defaults/configs. Each subsystem is a gated checkpoint with the functionality matrix re-verified before advancing.
   - **SD-004 LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion** (load_bearing: false): Hold for a separate A/B sprint outside this migration. Rationale: bundling a defaults flip into a code-move migration conflates "did resync break behavior" with "did new defaults change behavior"; violates single-variable diagnosis.
   - **SD-005 res_2s / self_refiner exposure** (load_bearing: false): Land as off-by-default flags in Sprint 3. Only flip defaults on after a dedicated A/B sprint.
   - **SD-006 Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion** (load_bearing: false): Out of migration scope. Separate initiative, explicitly deferred in Non-Goals. Rationale: brief calls it "independent of the fork migration"; self_refiner adoption may obviate it.
   - **SD-007 Post-migration rebase cadence** (load_bearing: true): Monthly rebase + event-triggered rebase on upstream minor version bumps (any `v10.X → v10.(X+1)` tag). Weekly is too noisy for a ~9-minor-behind baseline; quarterly re-drifts toward current problem.
   - **SD-008 Drift detection CI** (load_bearing: true): Add a scheduled GitHub Actions job `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` that runs weekly, shallow-clones upstream, computes `git diff --stat <fork-base>..<upstream-head> -- Wan2GP/`, and opens a tracking issue when drift exceeds N commits or touches a path in a curated hot-list (models/ltx2/, shared/self_refiner.py).
3. **Cross-reference** each SD from within Section 3 prose (e.g. "We adopt a hybrid fork+patch shape (see SD-001)").

  "signals": {
    "iteration": 1,
    "idea": "Produce a design document that plans the full migration of reigh-worker's vendored deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP code from its current drifted snapshot (~50 days / ~9 minor versions behind, no .git directory) into a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, broken into 2-week sprints. The document's job is to define the sprints, not to execute them.\n\nNon-negotiable constraint throughout every sprint: NO FUNCTIONALITY MAY BE LOST. Every behavior reigh-worker currently depends on \u2014 IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose/depth/canny/cameraman modes (source/core/params/travel_guidance.py _IC_LORA_BY_MODE, _auto_inject_travel_guidance_lora), the newly-landed ltx_anchor travel guidance kind, the existing ltx_control and ltx_hybrid travel paths, the vendored ICLoraPipeline behavior, the custom patches our vendored tree carries \u2014 must survive, be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan, and have a verification step.\n\nContext the document must incorporate (treat as authoritative background):\n\n- Current situation: reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ is a flat copy without a .git directory. Cannot be rebased. Upstream is deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP. Approximately 50 days behind (vendored top changelog entry is v10.01 / 2026-01-01; upstream top is v10.951 / 2026-02-19). 22 files differ in Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ alone, ~4303 diff lines.\n- Missing upstream features we want: ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json defaults, shared/utils/self_refiner.py (Feb 4), models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py (res_2s sampler), outpaint IC-LoRA + gamma preprocessing (_apply_gamma_to_media, LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA, LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO constants), prompt_enhancer improvements.\n- Upstream's IC-LoRA pattern is simpler than ours: single union-control preload URL for distilled 22B (models/ltx2/ltx2.py:783-832), substring-matched, no per-mode Python dict like our _IC_LORA_BY_MODE. Upstream auto-forces guide_phases=1 when P/D/E in video_prompt_type (ltx2.py:918). Cameraman-style per-mode LoRAs are 19B (not 22B). This means a large chunk of our custom IC-LoRA registry logic is either convergeable to upstream's approach or should become a thin override.\n- Detailer situation: LTX-2 detailer is 19B only (no 22B/2.3 variant exists). Lightricks' ICLoraPipeline runs LoRA stage-1-only with stage-2 latent chaining. Upstream has no LTX-2 detailer integration. self_refiner (PnP mid-denoise) is the nearest thing upstream offers and is likely to obviate inline detailing after we adopt it.\n- User preference: detailer, if we build it, should be a post-gen task handler (like source/task_handlers/rife_interpolate.py) rather than inline in the travel pipeline. This is independent of the fork migration but is a natural follow-up sprint.\n- Constraint inventory (what reigh-worker currently depends on that must survive):\n  - IC-LoRA auto-injection by mode (source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987) wiring union-control to pose/depth/canny and cameraman LoRA to cameraman mode.\n  - Pixel cross-fade guide video path for ltx_control (source/media/video/travel_guide.py) \u2014 must NOT be broken by the resync even though it's arguably legacy.\n  - ltx_anchor kind just shipped in commit 9656cc90 \u2014 must survive the resync. The rename _build_segment_hybrid_guidance_config \u2192 _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config touched vendored tests indirectly; verify.\n  - The vendored Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79 clear_conditioning that strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode \u2014 load-bearing for ltx_anchor, must not be accidentally reverted to an upstream version lacking it if we have patches here.\n  - IC-LoRA dedup logic (task_registry.py:961-968).\n  - Travel orchestrator PATH A skip for ltx_anchor (orchestrator.py:1278-1289) and _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config rename (orchestrator.py).\n  - Existing test suite: tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py (20 ltx_anchor tests), tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py (8 runtime tests incl. source tripwire), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py (23 tests, recently promoted to tracked via .gitignore allowlist), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py, tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py, tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py.\n\nOpen design decisions the document must resolve (as Settled Decisions with rationale):\n\n1. Fork shape: submodule pinned to upstream SHA vs. our own long-running fork on GitHub (e.g. banodoco/Wan2GP) vs. monkeypatch-only with upstream as a dep. Probably a hybrid \u2014 fork + minimal monkeypatch layer for things that shouldn't be in the fork. Document must pick one concretely.\n2. Patch triage buckets: every deviation between our vendored tree and upstream gets classified as (a) accept upstream \u2014 stale not intentional, (b) upstreamable \u2014 PR to deepbeepmeep, (c) banodoco-specific \u2014 carry in fork or as override, (d) cruft \u2014 drop. Document must define the triage process as a sprint task.\n3. Initial resync cadence: do we cut over to upstream head all at once, or stage by subsystem (e.g., models/ltx2/ first, then shared/, then postprocessing/)? Document must pick.\n4. LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion: bundle into the migration, or hold for a separate A/B sprint? Document must pick.\n5. res_2s / self_refiner exposure: off-by-default flags, or promote to default once upstream does? Document must pick.\n6. Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion: does this sit inside the fork migration sprint plan (as a sprint 3 or 4), or is it a separate initiative? Document must pick.\n7. Rebase cadence post-migration: weekly? Monthly? Triggered by specific upstream events? Document must pick.\n8. CI / drift detection: how do we avoid re-drifting once we're synced? Document must propose a specific mechanism (scheduled CI job comparing to upstream head, etc.).\n\nTarget shape of the final document:\n- Executive summary (what, why, how long, what we're protecting)\n- Current-state inventory (what's vendored, what's drifted, what's load-bearing)\n- Fork architecture decision (the Settled Decisions on shape, patch triage, cadence)\n- Sprint breakdown: 3-5 sprints of 2 weeks each, each with: goals, deliverables, verification gates, rollback plan, explicit functionality-preservation checks. First sprint should be inventory-heavy (the diff triage is where the risk lives). Last sprint should cover sustainability (rebase cadence, CI, detection).\n- Risk register: what could go wrong per sprint, probability, impact, mitigation\n- Success criteria: objective checks for migration complete vs migration in progress vs regression observed\n- Explicit Settled Decisions section using the canonical megaplan format (id: SD-NNN, load_bearing, decision, rationale)\n\nOut of scope (document should note these explicitly as non-goals): flipping ltx_anchor to default, real-GPU empirical validation of the new defaults, upscaler-pass anchor re-injection, identity-drift fixes.\n\nHonor the memory-note constraint: do NOT include hard-halt prerequisite clauses of the form halt when MX not shipped.         Requirements:
        - Before addressing individual flags, check: does any flag suggest the plan is targeting the wrong code or the wrong root cause? If so, consider whether the plan needs a new approach rather than adjustments. Explain your reasoning.
        - Update the plan to address the significant issues.
        - Keep the plan readable and executable.
        - Return flags_addressed with the exact flag IDs you addressed.
        - Include `changes_summary` as a short plain-English summary of what changed in the revision. If there were no concrete flags, say that explicitly (for example: `No critique flags were raised; refined wording and kept the plan aligned for execution.`).
        - Preserve or improve success criteria quality. Each criterion must have a `priority` of `must`, `should`, or `info`. Promote or demote priorities if critique feedback reveals a criterion was over- or under-weighted.
        - Verify that the plan remains aligned with the user's original intent, not just internal plan quality.
        - Remove unjustified scope growth. If critique raised scope creep, narrow the plan back to the original idea unless the broader work is strictly required.
        - Maintain the structural template: H1 title, ## Overview, phase sections with numbered step sections, ## Execution Order or ## Validation Order.
        - CRITICAL: Your entire revised plan markdown (all sections) must be output as the `plan` field in the structured output. The prose response must not contain the plan text.
        - CRITICAL: Return only the structured JSON object for the schema fields `plan`, `changes_summary`, `flags_addressed`, `assumptions`, `success_criteria`, and `questions`. Do not add commentary before or after the JSON object.

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

Revised resolutions (reuse existing seams, preserve path contract):\n   - **SD-001 Fork shape** (load_bearing: true): Hybrid — `banodoco/Wan2GP` long-running fork on GitHub, consumed as a git submodule **mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`** (same filesystem path as today, preserving the 26+ `Wan2GP.*` import call sites). Deviations that don't belong in the fork are **added to the existing `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` module** (which is already auto-imported by `source/__init__.py:6` and already offers `begin/apply/rollback_runtime_model_patch` via `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`). No new parallel patch package. A new submodule inside `wgp_patches` named `wan2gp_ltx2_patches` (same module, logical namespace) may be introduced if the file grows; no new top-level runtime import hook. Rationale: (a) pure submodule loses monkeypatch agility for narrow overrides; (b) pure monkeypatch can't carry file-level forks like `ltx_core/tools.py:clear_conditioning`; (c) a parallel `source/runtime/wan2gp_patches/` would duplicate the existing bootstrap and split the patch registry. Reusing `wgp_patches.py` keeps rollback/state tracking unified.\n   - **SD-002 Patch triage buckets** (load_bearing: true): Four buckets per diff hunk — (a) accept-upstream (stale drift), (b) upstreamable (PR to deepbeepmeep), (c) banodoco-fork (carry in fork branch) or banodoco-patch (carry as `wgp_patches` entry), (d) cruft (drop). Triage CSV lives at `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` with columns `path,hunk,bucket,carry_location,owner,verification`. The `carry_location` column distinguishes fork-file vs `wgp_patches` entry for bucket (c).\n   - **SD-003 Resync cadence** (load_bearing: true): Stage by subsystem. Order: `shared/` (including self_refiner drift check) + `postprocessing/` first → `models/ltx2/` (the 22-file hot zone with `clear_conditioning`) → defaults/configs. Each subsystem is a gated checkpoint with functionality matrix re-verified before advancing.\n   - **SD-004 LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion** (load_bearing: false): Hold for a separate A/B sprint. Bundling a defaults flip into a code-move migration conflates \"did resync break behavior\" with \"did new defaults change behavior\".\n   - **SD-005 res_2s / self_refiner exposure** (load_bearing: false): Land `res2s` (genuinely missing, backfill) and the upstream self_refiner (drift-upgrade) behind off-by-default flags in Sprint 2/3. Flip defaults only after a dedicated A/B sprint.\n   - **SD-006 Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion** (load_bearing: false): Out of migration scope. Mentioned in §8 Non-Goals. Rationale: brief calls it \"independent of the fork migration\"; self_refiner adoption may obviate it.\n   - **SD-007 Post-migration rebase cadence** (load_bearing: true): Monthly rebase + event-triggered rebase on upstream minor version bumps (`v10.X → v10.(X+1)`). Weekly is noise for a ~9-minor-behind baseline; quarterly re-drifts.\n   - **SD-008 Drift detection CI** (load_bearing: true): Scheduled GitHub Actions `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` (in reigh-worker, not the fork) runs weekly, shallow-clones upstream, runs `git diff --stat <fork-base>..<upstream-head> -- Wan2GP/`, and opens a tracking issue when drift exceeds N commits or touches curated hot paths (`models/ltx2/`, `shared/utils/self_refiner.py`, `models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py`).\n   - **SD-009 Path/import contract preservation** (load_bearing: true) — **new SD added to address the hard-coded `Wan2GP.*` surface**: the submodule MUST mount at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` and expose the same `Wan2GP.*` Python import root. `source/core/runtime_paths.py:ensure_wan2gp_on_path` must continue to resolve. Any rename or relocation is explicitly prohibited during this migration; if a future migration needs it, that is a separate initiative.\n3. **Cross-reference** each SD from within §3 prose.\n\nNote: the decision count is now 9 (SD-001..SD-009). This is a deliberate expansion to address critique FLAG-002 / all_locations-1 / all_locations-2. If the reviewer requires exactly 8, SD-009 can be folded into SD-001's rationale, but keeping it separate makes the path/import contract auditable as a first-class invariant.\n\n### Step 4: Write the sprint plan (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §4)\n**Scope:** Large\n1. **Structure** 4 sprints × 2 weeks. Each sprint contains: Goals / Deliverables / Verification Gates / Rollback Plan / Functionality-Preservation Checks. **Rollback uses tag-based anchors, not commit SHAs** — each sprint opens by creating a git tag `wan2gp-mig-sprint-{N}-baseline` against the current reigh-worker HEAD at sprint start. ### Step 3: Resolve the 8 open design decisions (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §3, §9)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Write** §9 in canonical megaplan format:
   ```
   ### SD-001
   - load_bearing: true|false
   - decision: <one-sentence resolution>
   - rationale: <2-4 sentences>
   ```
2. **Resolve** the 8 decisions. Revised resolutions (reuse existing seams, preserve path contract):
   - **SD-001 Fork shape** (load_bearing: true): Hybrid — `banodoco/Wan2GP` long-running fork on GitHub, consumed as a git submodule **mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`** (same filesystem path as today, preserving the 26+ `Wan2GP.*` import call sites). Deviations that don't belong in the fork are **added to the existing `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` module** (which is already auto-imported by `source/__init__.py:6` and already offers `begin/apply/rollback_runtime_model_patch` via `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`). No new parallel patch package. A new submodule inside `wgp_patches` named `wan2gp_ltx2_patches` (same module, logical namespace) may be introduced if the file grows; no new top-level runtime import hook. Rationale: (a) pure submodule loses monkeypatch agility for narrow overrides; (b) pure monkeypatch can't carry file-level forks like `ltx_core/tools.py:clear_conditioning`; (c) a parallel `source/runtime/wan2gp_patches/` would duplicate the existing bootstrap and split the patch registry. Reusing `wgp_patches.py` keeps rollback/state tracking unified.
   - **SD-002 Patch triage buckets** (load_bearing: true): Four buckets per diff hunk — (a) accept-upstream (stale drift), (b) upstreamable (PR to deepbeepmeep), (c) banodoco-fork (carry in fork branch) or banodoco-patch (carry as `wgp_patches` entry), (d) cruft (drop). Triage CSV lives at `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` with columns `path,hunk,bucket,carry_location,owner,verification`. The `carry_location` column distinguishes fork-file vs `wgp_patches` entry for bucket (c).
   - **SD-003 Resync cadence** (load_bearing: true): Stage by subsystem. Order: `shared/` (including self_refiner drift check) + `postprocessing/` first → `models/ltx2/` (the 22-file hot zone with `clear_conditioning`) → defaults/configs. Each subsystem is a gated checkpoint with functionality matrix re-verified before advancing.

   - **SD-005 res_2s / self_refiner exposure** (load_bearing: false): Land `res2s` (genuinely missing, backfill) and the upstream self_refiner (drift-upgrade) behind off-by-default flags in Sprint 2/3. Flip defaults only after a dedicated A/B sprint.
   - **SD-006 Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion** (load_bearing: false): Out of migration scope. Mentioned in §8 Non-Goals. Rationale: brief calls it "independent of the fork migration"; self_refiner adoption may obviate it.
   - **SD-007 Post-migration rebase cadence** (load_bearing: true): Monthly rebase + event-triggered rebase on upstream minor version bumps (`v10.X → v10.(X+1)`). Weekly is noise for a ~9-minor-behind baseline; quarterly re-drifts.
   - **SD-008 Drift detection CI** (load_bearing: true): Scheduled GitHub Actions `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` (in reigh-worker, not the fork) runs weekly, shallow-clones upstream, runs `git diff --stat <fork-base>..<upstream-head> -- Wan2GP/`, and opens a tracking issue when drift exceeds N commits or touches curated hot paths (`models/ltx2/`, `shared/utils/self_refiner.py`, `models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py`).
   - **SD-009 Path/import contract preservation** (load_bearing: true) — **new SD added to address the hard-coded `Wan2GP.*` surface**: the submodule MUST mount at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` and expose the same `Wan2GP.*` Python import root. `source/core/runtime_paths.py:ensure_wan2gp_on_path` must continue to resolve. Any rename or relocation is explicitly prohibited during this migration; if a future migration needs it, that is a separate initiative.

4. **Sprint 3 — `models/ltx2/` Resync (Weeks 5-6)**
   - Goals: resync the 22-file hot zone; carry `clear_conditioning` and other bucket-(c) fork hunks into the fork branch; pick up `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` as available-but-not-default (SD-004); reconcile upstream's `guide_phases=1` auto-force (ltx2.py:918) with our pipeline.
   - Deliverables: `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:clear_conditioning` byte-identical to pre-sprint behavior on a saved latent fixture; upstream union-control preload coexists with our per-mode `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` (cameraman) plus our `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` fallback branch at `travel_guidance.py:305-325`; dedup at `task_registry.py:961-968` still fires.
   - Verification gates: IC-LoRA integration test split into **two distinct cases** — (a) pose/depth/canny mode → verify the union-control fallback path in `get_ic_lora_entry()` still returns `{"path": _IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL, ...}` and preload fires; (b) cameraman mode → verify the `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` override branch returns the Cseti URL-bearing entry and the dedup at `task_registry.py:964-968` correctly updates strength if that LoRA was already in `segment_loras`. `pytest -k 'anchor or ic_lora'`; byte-compare `clear_conditioning` output on a saved latent fixture against pre-resync; `test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py` covers PATH A skip at `travel/orchestrator.py:1272-1289`; `test_travel_guidance_config.py` (20 anchor tests) passes.
   - Rollback: revert submodule pointer to Sprint-2 SHA; `git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-3-baseline` for any reigh-worker side wgp_patches additions.
   - Functionality-preservation checks (explicit, distinguishing fallback from override): (i) **union-control fallback** at `travel_guidance.py:314` for pose/depth/canny still returns `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL`; (ii) **cameraman override** at `travel_guidance.py:310-313` still returns the URL-bearing entry with `name: "ic-lora-cameraman (auto-injected)"`; (iii) ltx_anchor PATH A skip at `travel/orchestrator.py:1278` still fires; (iv) `ltx_control` pixel cross-fade guide (`source/media/video/travel_guide.py`) still produces frames; (v) `ltx_hybrid` unchanged; (vi) `clear_conditioning` strips keyframe tokens before VAE decode (not reverted to an upstream-lacking version); (vii) all 26+ `Wan2GP.*` import call sites still resolve.
5. **Sprint 4 — Patch-Layer Consolidation, Upstreamable PRs, Sustainability (Weeks 7-8)**
   - Goals: add Wan2GP-specific patches to the **existing `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py`** module (or a sibling module inside the same import graph registered through the same `source/__init__.py` bootstrap — NOT a new `source/runtime/wan2gp_patches/` parallel) for any bucket-(c) items that are easier to carry as runtime patches than as fork files; file upstream PRs for bucket-(b); land drift-detection CI (SD-008); publish rebase runbook (SD-007).
   - Deliverables: `wgp_patches.py` extended (or sibling module in same bootstrap) with documented Wan2GP patches using the existing `_register_patch_application` / `begin_runtime_model_patch` / `rollback_runtime_model_patch` primitives; draft PRs filed for bucket-(b); `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` merged; `docs/wan2gp-rebase-runbook.md` published.
   - Verification gates: drift CI runs once successfully; full 6-file test suite green; patch layer covered by a smoke test that asserts `get_wgp_patch_state()` shows the Wan2GP patches applied at import time (reuses existing state-inspection API at `wgp_patches.py:60`).
   - Rollback: patch additions are isolated to `wgp_patches.py` — revert via `git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-4-baseline` or disable via existing context-rollback mechanism in `_PATCH_CONTEXT_ROLLBACKS`.
   - Functionality-preservation checks: end-to-end regression matrix from Sprint 3 re-run against submodule + extended `wgp_patches.py`; assert no patch collides with existing entries in `_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE`.

### Step 5: Write the Functionality Preservation Matrix (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §5)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Produce** a table with columns `Behavior | Defining location | Sprint verifying it | Verification step`. Rows (corrected: IC-LoRA has TWO rows, fallback and override):
   - IC-LoRA **union-control fallback** (pose/depth/canny) → `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:314`, `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987` → Sprint 3 → pytest case asserting `get_ic_lora_entry()` returns `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` for `mode in {pose,depth,canny}`.
   - IC-LoRA **cameraman override** → `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:310-313` + `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE:32-37` → Sprint 3 → pytest case asserting `get_ic_lora_entry()` for `mode=cameraman` returns the Cseti URL-bearing entry; dedup at `task_registry.py:964-968` updates strength on duplicate.
   - Pixel cross-fade guide for ltx_control → `source/media/video/travel_guide.py` → Sprint 3 → `test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py` + manual ltx_control smoke.
   - ltx_anchor kind → `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py:134,231,1272-1289,2115,2126` → Sprint 3 → `test_travel_guidance_config.py` (20 anchor tests).
   - `clear_conditioning` keyframe strip → `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79` → Sprint 3 → byte-compare latent fixture.
   - IC-LoRA dedup → `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:961-968` → Sprint 3 → targeted unit test.
   - PATH A skip for ltx_anchor → `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py:1272-1289` → Sprint 3 → `test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py`.
   - `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config` rename → `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py:178` → Sprint 3 → `test_travel_guidance_config.py`.
   - `Wan2GP/` path + `Wan2GP.*` import root (SD-009) → `source/core/runtime_paths.py:ensure_wan2gp_on_path` + 26+ call sites → Sprint 2 → smoke test that imports a representative `Wan2GP.*` module and asserts `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` returns successfully.
   - self_refiner drift upgrade → `Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` → Sprint 2 → explicit LTX-2 smoke test (self_refiner is already active in LTX-2 codepaths).
   - `wgp_patches` patch-state integrity → `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` + `source/__init__.py:6` → Sprint 4 → assert `get_wgp_patch_state()` shows expected entries and no collisions.
   - Full 6-file test suite → `tests/` → every sprint → `pytest` named files.

### Step 6: Write Risk Register and Success Criteria (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §6, §7)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Risk register** rows `Risk | Sprint | Probability | Impact | Mitigation`. Minimum entries:
   - Triage misclassifies banodoco-specific hunk as upstream-accept → S1 → M/H → 10% sample spot-check + second-pass review.
   - `clear_conditioning` silently reverted to upstream-lacking version → S3 → M/H → byte-compare latent fixture.
   - IC-LoRA per-mode registry collides with upstream union-control preload → S3 → H/M → explicit separate tests for fallback vs override paths (see §5).
   - Submodule mount breaks the `Wan2GP.*` import root or `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` filesystem path → S2 → M/H → SD-009 invariant + import-resolution smoke test in Sprint 2 gate.
   - New patch package runs parallel to `wgp_patches.py` → S4 → M/M → SD-001 forbids a new package; patch smoke test asserts `get_wgp_patch_state()` shows a single registry.
   - self_refiner version bump changes LTX-2 behavior silently → S2 → M/M → LTX-2 smoke test in Sprint 2 gate.
   - README/STRUCTURE.md stay stale after submodule cut-over → S2 → L/L → deliverable explicitly includes doc updates.
   - Monthly rebase slips, drift returns → ongoing → M/M → SD-008 drift CI opens issue on threshold.
2. **Success criteria** — three objective states: (a) **Migration complete** = submodule pointer at upstream HEAD within monthly cadence AND 6 named test files pass AND drift CI green AND `wgp_patches.py` single-registry smoke passes; (b) **Migration in progress** = submodule mounted, subset of subsystems synced, full test suite green at each checkpoint; (c) **Regression observed** = any row in §5 verification step fails OR any of the 6 named test files regresses OR `Wan2GP.*` import resolution fails.

### Step 7: Verify the document (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Grep** for forbidden patterns: `halt when`, `halt if`, `force-proceed`, `do not proceed until` — none should match.
2. **Grep** for commit-SHA rollback anchors: no occurrences of 7+-char hex SHAs as rollback targets; only `wan2gp-mig-sprint-N-baseline` tag references.
3. **Grep** for `source/runtime/wan2gp_patches` — zero occurrences (parallel patch package forbidden).
4. **Grep** confirms mentions of the existing seam: `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py`, `source/__init__.py:6`, `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py` each appear at least once (proving SD-001 reuses rather than replaces).
5. **Grep** confirms each constraint-inventory name appears: `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE`, `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL`, `get_ic_lora_entry`, `ltx_anchor`, `ltx_control`, `ltx_hybrid`, `clear_conditioning`, `travel_guide`, `task_registry`, `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config`, `ensure_wan2gp_on_path`.
6. **Count** Settled Decisions — expect 9 (SD-001..SD-009) OR exactly 8 if SD-009 has been folded into SD-001 per reviewer preference.
7. **Count** sprints in §4 — expect 4.
8. **Confirm** §8 contains all four brief-specified non-goals.
9. **Confirm** §2c explicitly states `self_refiner.py` is already vendored (not listed as missing).

## Execution Order
1. Step 1 (re-inspection) first — catches any drift between revision time and execution time.
2. Steps 2 → 3 → 4 → 5 → 6 in order; later sections depend on Settled Decisions being fixed first.
3. Step 7 last — validates the document against all critique-raised invariants.

## Validation Order
1. Cheap checks first: Step 7's greps (forbidden patterns, SHA absence, parallel-package absence, required tokens).
2. Reviewer read-through for prose coherence, that each sprint's functionality-preservation check is specific (names behaviors/tests), and that the IC-LoRA matrix row split (fallback vs override) is prose-consistent.
3. Cross-reference §3 prose against §9 SDs — every SD cited in prose; every cited SD defined.
4. Human sign-off on SD resolutions — this is planning output, not execution authorization.

  "robustness": "standard",
  "signals": {
    "iteration": 2,
    "idea": "Produce a design document that plans the full migration of reigh-worker's vendored deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP code from its current drifted snapshot (~50 days / ~9 minor versions behind, no .git directory) into a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, broken into 2-week sprints. The document's job is to define the sprints, not to execute them.\n\nNon-negotiable constraint throughout every sprint: NO FUNCTIONALITY MAY BE LOST. Every behavior reigh-worker currently depends on \u2014 IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose/depth/canny/cameraman modes (source/core/params/travel_guidance.py _IC_LORA_BY_MODE, _auto_inject_travel_guidance_lora), the newly-landed ltx_anchor travel guidance kind, the existing ltx_control and ltx_hybrid travel paths, the vendored ICLoraPipeline behavior, the custom patches our vendored tree carries \u2014 must survive, be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan, and have a verification step.\n\nContext the document must incorporate (treat as authoritative background):\n\n- Current situation: reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ is a flat copy without a .git directory. Cannot be rebased. Upstream is deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP. Approximately 50 days behind (vendored top changelog entry is v10.01 / 2026-01-01; upstream top is v10.951 / 2026-02-19). 22 files differ in Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ alone, ~4303 diff lines.\n- Missing upstream features we want: ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json defaults, shared/utils/self_refiner.py (Feb 4), models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py (res_2s sampler), outpaint IC-LoRA + gamma preprocessing (_apply_gamma_to_media, LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA, LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO constants), prompt_enhancer improvements.\n- Upstream's IC-LoRA pattern is simpler than ours: single union-control preload URL for distilled 22B (models/ltx2/ltx2.py:783-832), substring-matched, no per-mode Python dict like our _IC_LORA_BY_MODE. Upstream auto-forces guide_phases=1 when P/D/E in video_prompt_type (ltx2.py:918). Cameraman-style per-mode LoRAs are 19B (not 22B). This means a large chunk of our custom IC-LoRA registry logic is either convergeable to upstream's approach or should become a thin override.\n- Detailer situation: LTX-2 detailer is 19B only (no 22B/2.3 variant exists). Lightricks' ICLoraPipeline runs LoRA stage-1-only with stage-2 latent chaining. Upstream has no LTX-2 detailer integration. self_refiner (PnP mid-denoise) is the nearest thing upstream offers and is likely to obviate inline detailing after we adopt it.\n- User preference: detailer, if we build it, should be a post-gen task handler (like source/task_handlers/rife_interpolate.py) rather than inline in the travel pipeline. This is independent of the fork migration but is a natural follow-up sprint.\n- Constraint inventory (what reigh-worker currently depends on that must survive):\n  - IC-LoRA auto-injection by mode (source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987) wiring union-control to pose/depth/canny and cameraman LoRA to cameraman mode.\n  - Pixel cross-fade guide video path for ltx_control (source/media/video/travel_guide.py) \u2014 must NOT be broken by the resync even though it's arguably legacy.\n  - ltx_anchor kind just shipped in commit 9656cc90 \u2014 must survive the resync. The rename _build_segment_hybrid_guidance_config \u2192 _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config touched vendored tests indirectly; verify.\n  - The vendored Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79 clear_conditioning that strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode \u2014 load-bearing for ltx_anchor, must not be accidentally reverted to an upstream version lacking it if we have patches here.\n  - IC-LoRA dedup logic (task_registry.py:961-968).\n  - Travel orchestrator PATH A skip for ltx_anchor (orchestrator.py:1278-1289) and _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config rename (orchestrator.py).\n  - Existing test suite: tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py (20 ltx_anchor tests), tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py (8 runtime tests incl. source tripwire), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py (23 tests, recently promoted to tracked via .gitignore allowlist), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py, tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py, tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py.\n\nOpen design decisions the document must resolve (as Settled Decisions with rationale):\n\n1. Fork shape: submodule pinned to upstream SHA vs. our own long-running fork on GitHub (e.g. banodoco/Wan2GP) vs. monkeypatch-only with upstream as a dep. Probably a hybrid \u2014 fork + minimal monkeypatch layer for things that shouldn't be in the fork. Document must pick one concretely.\n2. Patch triage buckets: every deviation between our vendored tree and upstream gets classified as (a) accept upstream \u2014 stale not intentional, (b) upstreamable \u2014 PR to deepbeepmeep, (c) banodoco-specific \u2014 carry in fork or as override, (d) cruft \u2014 drop. Document must define the triage process as a sprint task.\n3. Initial resync cadence: do we cut over to upstream head all at once, or stage by subsystem (e.g., models/ltx2/ first, then shared/, then postprocessing/)? Document must pick.\n4. LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion: bundle into the migration, or hold for a separate A/B sprint? Document must pick.\n5. res_2s / self_refiner exposure: off-by-default flags, or promote to default once upstream does? Document must pick.\n6. Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion: does this sit inside the fork migration sprint plan (as a sprint 3 or 4), or is it a separate initiative? Document must pick.\n7. Rebase cadence post-migration: weekly? Monthly? Triggered by specific upstream events? Document must pick.\n8. CI / drift detection: how do we avoid re-drifting once we're synced? Document must propose a specific mechanism (scheduled CI job comparing to upstream head, etc.).\n\nTarget shape of the final document:\n- Executive summary (what, why, how long, what we're protecting)\n- Current-state inventory (what's vendored, what's drifted, what's load-bearing)\n- Fork architecture decision (the Settled Decisions on shape, patch triage, cadence)\n- Sprint breakdown: 3-5 sprints of 2 weeks each, each with: goals, deliverables, verification gates, rollback plan, explicit functionality-preservation checks. First sprint should be inventory-heavy (the diff triage is where the risk lives). Last sprint should cover sustainability (rebase cadence, CI, detection).\n- Risk register: what could go wrong per sprint, probability, impact, mitigation\n- Success criteria: objective checks for migration complete vs migration in progress vs regression observed\n- Explicit Settled Decisions section using the canonical megaplan format (id: SD-NNN, load_bearing, decision, rationale)\n\nOut of scope (document should note these explicitly as non-goals): flipping ltx_anchor to default, real-GPU empirical validation of the new defaults, upscaler-pass anchor re-injection, identity-drift fixes.\n\nHonor the memory-note constraint: do NOT include hard-halt prerequisite clauses of the form halt when MX not shipped. Each sprint must be able to proceed, revise scope, or explicitly deprioritize \u2014 but not sit in force-proceed limbo.",

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

> AGENT

Context the document must incorporate (treat as authoritative background):

- Current situation: reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ is a flat copy without a .git directory. Cannot be rebased. Upstream is deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP. Upstream auto-forces guide_phases=1 when P/D/E in video_prompt_type (ltx2.py:918). Cameraman-style per-mode LoRAs are 19B (not 22B). This means a large chunk of our custom IC-LoRA registry logic is either convergeable to upstream's approach or should become a thin override.
- Detailer situation: LTX-2 detailer is 19B only (no 22B/2.3 variant exists). Lightricks' ICLoraPipeline runs LoRA stage-1-only with stage-2 latent chaining. Upstream has no LTX-2 detailer integration. self_refiner (PnP mid-denoise) is the nearest thing upstream offers and is likely to obviate inline detailing after we adopt it.
- User preference: detailer, if we build it, should be a post-gen task handler (like source/task_handlers/rife_interpolate.py) rather than inline in the travel pipeline. This is independent of the fork migration but is a natural follow-up sprint.

        # Implementation Plan: Wan2GP Fork Migration Design Document (Revision 3)

## Overview
Produce a design document at `reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` that plans (does **not** execute) the migration of reigh-worker's vendored, `.git`-less Wan2GP snapshot (vendored changelog top: `v10.01 / 2026-01-01` per `Wan2GP/docs/CHANGELOG.md`; upstream head: `v10.951 / 2026-02-19`; ~9 minor versions / ~50 days / 22 diffed files in `models/ltx2/` / ~4303 diff lines) into a fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, organized as 4 two-week sprints, with **exactly 8 Settled Decisions** (SD-001..SD-008) per the brief's contract.

Non-negotiable: **no functionality loss**. Every reigh-worker behavior in the brief's constraint inventory (IC-LoRA auto-injection via both the `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` override and the `_IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL` fallback, `ltx_anchor`/`ltx_hybrid`/`ltx_control` travel kinds, `clear_conditioning`, pixel cross-fade guide, test suite, `Wan2GP/` filesystem path, `Wan2GP.*` import root) must be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan with a verification step.

**Repo state verified at revision time**:
- `Wan2GP/docs/CHANGELOG.md` (top entry `v10.01 / January 1st 2026`).
- `Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` **is already vendored** — not missing. Treat as drift candidate.
- `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py` genuinely missing; `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` genuinely missing; grep finds zero `_apply_gamma_to_media` / `LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA` / `LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO`.

Patch state in `_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE` is populated *at that call site*, not at import time. `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py` exposes `begin/apply/rollback_runtime_model_patch` via `runtime_registry`.

All three need updating in the cut-over sprint; a `rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md` sweep catches any other occurrence.

### Step 3: Resolve the 8 open design decisions (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §3, §9)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Write** §9 in canonical megaplan format (exactly 8 decisions):
   ```
   ### SD-001
   - load_bearing: true|false
   - decision: <one-sentence resolution>
   - rationale: <2-4 sentences>
   ```
2. **Resolve** the 8 decisions. The path/import-contract invariant is folded back into SD-001 as a named sub-clause so the SD count matches the brief.
   - **SD-001 Fork shape (including path/import contract invariant)** (load_bearing: true): Hybrid — `banodoco/Wan2GP` long-running fork on GitHub, consumed as a git submodule **mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`** (same filesystem path as today, preserving the 26+ `Wan2GP.*` import call sites and `source/core/runtime_paths.py:ensure_wan2gp_on_path`). **Invariant clause (path/import contract)**: the submodule MUST mount at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` and expose the same `Wan2GP.*` Python import root; any rename/relocation is out of scope and would be a separate initiative. Deviations that don't belong in the fork are added to the existing `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` module (imported via `source/__init__.py:6`; applied via `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`). No new parallel patch package (no `source/runtime/wan2gp_patches/`). Rationale: (a) pure submodule loses monkeypatch agility; (b) pure monkeypatch can't carry file-level forks like `ltx_core/tools.py:clear_conditioning`; (c) a parallel patch package would split the patch registry — reusing `wgp_patches.py` keeps `_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE` / `_PATCH_CONTEXT_ROLLBACKS` unified and respects the verified lifecycle (patch state only populated *after* `apply_all_wgp_patches` runs).
   - **SD-002 Patch triage buckets** (load_bearing: true): Four buckets per diff hunk — (a) accept-upstream, (b) upstreamable (PR to deepbeepmeep), (c) banodoco-fork (carry in fork) or banodoco-patch (add to `wgp_patches.py`), (d) cruft. Triage CSV at `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv`, columns `path,hunk,bucket,carry_location,owner,verification`.
   - **SD-003 Resync cadence** (load_bearing: true): Stage by subsystem. Order: `shared/` (including self_refiner drift) + `postprocessing/` → `models/ltx2/` (22-file hot zone including `clear_conditioning`) → defaults/configs. Each subsystem is a gated checkpoint.
   - **SD-004 LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion** (load_bearing: false): Hold for a separate A/B sprint. Rationale: don't conflate "did resync break behavior" with "did new defaults change behavior".
   - **SD-005 res_2s / self_refiner exposure** (load_bearing: false): Land res2s backfill and self_refiner drift-upgrade behind off-by-default flags in Sprints 2/3. Flip defaults only after a dedicated A/B sprint.
   - **SD-006 Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion** (load_bearing: false): Out of migration scope. Listed in §8 Non-Goals. Rationale: brief calls it independent; self_refiner may obviate it.
   - **SD-007 Post-migration rebase cadence** (load_bearing: true): Monthly + event-triggered on upstream minor version bumps (`v10.X → v10.(X+1)`).
   - **SD-008 Drift detection CI** (load_bearing: true): `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` in reigh-worker (not the fork), weekly, shallow-clones upstream, runs `git diff --stat <fork-base>..<upstream-head> -- Wan2GP/`, opens tracking issue when drift exceeds threshold or touches curated hot paths (`models/ltx2/`, `shared/utils/self_refiner.py`, `models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py`).
3. **Cross-reference** each SD from §3 prose.

### Step 4: Write the sprint plan (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §4)

Rollback uses tag-based anchors (`wan2gp-mig-sprint-{N}-baseline`) created at sprint start. **All test invocations use `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`** (plain `pytest` fails `ModuleNotFoundError: source`). Forbidden: "halt when …", "force-proceed if …".

### Step 6: Write Risk Register and Success Criteria (`reigh-worker/docs/wan2gp-fork-migration-plan.md` §6, §7)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Risk register** — `Risk | Sprint | Probability | Impact | Mitigation`:
   - Triage misclassifies banodoco-specific hunk as upstream-accept → S1 → M/H → 10% spot-check + second-pass review.
   - `clear_conditioning` silently reverted → S3 → M/H → byte-compare latent fixture.
   - IC-LoRA registry collides with upstream union-control preload → S3 → H/M → separate pose/depth/canny fallback and cameraman override tests.
   - Submodule mount breaks the `Wan2GP.*` import root or filesystem path → S2 → M/H → SD-001 invariant + import-resolution smoke test.
   - New patch package would run parallel to `wgp_patches.py` → S4 → M/M → SD-001 forbids new package; patch smoke test asserts `_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE` has a single registry.
   - Patch-state smoke test anchored at wrong lifecycle point → S4 → M/M → test must drive orchestrator bootstrap to `orchestrator.py:265-266` before inspecting state.
   - self_refiner version bump changes LTX-2 behavior silently → S2 → M/M → LTX-2 smoke test in Sprint 2 gate.
   - Stale docs remain after cut-over → S2 → L/M → `rg "vendored.*subtree"` sweep must return empty as a Sprint 2 gate.
   - Verification commands fail to run because of environment contract → any sprint → M/H → all `pytest` invocations use `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`; no plain `pytest`.
   - Monthly rebase slips, drift returns → ongoing → M/M → SD-008 drift CI.
2. **Success criteria** — three objective states: (a) **Migration complete** = submodule pointer at upstream HEAD within monthly cadence AND 6 named test files pass under `uv run --python 3.10 pytest` AND drift CI green AND post-bootstrap `get_wgp_patch_state()` smoke passes; (b) **Migration in progress** = submodule mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`, subset of subsystems synced, full test suite green at each checkpoint, stale-doc sweep empty; (c) **Regression observed** = any row in §5 verification step fails OR any of the 6 named test files regresses OR `Wan2GP.*` import resolution fails OR `rg "vendored.*subtree"` returns a hit post-Sprint-2.

  "signals": {
    "iteration": 3,
    "idea": "Produce a design document that plans the full migration of reigh-worker's vendored deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP code from its current drifted snapshot (~50 days / ~9 minor versions behind, no .git directory) into a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, broken into 2-week sprints. The document's job is to define the sprints, not to execute them.\n\nNon-negotiable constraint throughout every sprint: NO FUNCTIONALITY MAY BE LOST. Every behavior reigh-worker currently depends on \u2014 IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose/depth/canny/cameraman modes (source/core/params/travel_guidance.py _IC_LORA_BY_MODE, _auto_inject_travel_guidance_lora), the newly-landed ltx_anchor travel guidance kind, the existing ltx_control and ltx_hybrid travel paths, the vendored ICLoraPipeline behavior, the custom patches our vendored tree carries \u2014 must survive, be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan, and have a verification step.\n\nContext the document must incorporate (treat as authoritative background):\n\n- Current situation: reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ is a flat copy without a .git directory. Cannot be rebased. Upstream is deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP. Approximately 50 days behind (vendored top changelog entry is v10.01 / 2026-01-01; upstream top is v10.951 / 2026-02-19). 22 files differ in Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ alone, ~4303 diff lines.\n- Missing upstream features we want: ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json defaults, shared/utils/self_refiner.py (Feb 4), models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py (res_2s sampler), outpaint IC-LoRA + gamma preprocessing (_apply_gamma_to_media, LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA, LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO constants), prompt_enhancer improvements.\n- Upstream's IC-LoRA pattern is simpler than ours: single union-control preload URL for distilled 22B (models/ltx2/ltx2.py:783-832), substring-matched, no per-mode Python dict like our _IC_LORA_BY_MODE. Upstream auto-forces guide_phases=1 when P/D/E in video_prompt_type (ltx2.py:918). Cameraman-style per-mode LoRAs are 19B (not 22B). This means a large chunk of our custom IC-LoRA registry logic is either convergeable to upstream's approach or should become a thin override.\n- Detailer situation: LTX-2 detailer is 19B only (no 22B/2.3 variant exists). Lightricks' ICLoraPipeline runs LoRA stage-1-only with stage-2 latent chaining. Upstream has no LTX-2 detailer integration. self_refiner (PnP mid-denoise) is the nearest thing upstream offers and is likely to obviate inline detailing after we adopt it.\n- User preference: detailer, if we build it, should be a post-gen task handler (like source/task_handlers/rife_interpolate.py) rather than inline in the travel pipeline. This is independent of the fork migration but is a natural follow-up sprint.\n- Constraint inventory (what reigh-worker currently depends on that must survive):\n  - IC-LoRA auto-injection by mode (source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987) wiring union-control to pose/depth/canny and cameraman LoRA to cameraman mode.\n  - Pixel cross-fade guide video path for ltx_control (source/media/video/travel_guide.py) \u2014 must NOT be broken by the resync even though it's arguably legacy.\n  - ltx_anchor kind just shipped in commit 9656cc90 \u2014 must survive the resync. The rename _build_segment_hybrid_guidance_config \u2192 _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config touched vendored tests indirectly; verify.\n  - The vendored Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79 clear_conditioning that strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode \u2014 load-bearing for ltx_anchor, must not be accidentally reverted to an upstream version lacking it if we have patches here.\n  - IC-LoRA dedup logic (task_registry.py:961-968).\n  - Travel orchestrator PATH A skip for ltx_anchor (orchestrator.py:1278-1289) and _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config rename (orchestrator.py).\n  - Existing test suite: tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py (20 ltx_anchor tests), tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py (8 runtime tests incl. source tripwire), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py (23 tests, recently promoted to tracked via .gitignore allowlist), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py, tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py, tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py.\n\nOpen design decisions the document must resolve (as Settled Decisions with rationale):\n\n1. Fork shape: submodule pinned to upstream SHA vs. our own long-running fork on GitHub (e.g. banodoco/Wan2GP) vs. monkeypatch-only with upstream as a dep. Probably a hybrid \u2014 fork + minimal monkeypatch layer for things that shouldn't be in the fork. Document must pick one concretely.\n2. Patch triage buckets: every deviation between our vendored tree and upstream gets classified as (a) accept upstream \u2014 stale not intentional, (b) upstreamable \u2014 PR to deepbeepmeep, (c) banodoco-specific \u2014 carry in fork or as override, (d) cruft \u2014 drop. Document must define the triage process as a sprint task.\n3. Initial resync cadence: do we cut over to upstream head all at once, or stage by subsystem (e.g., models/ltx2/ first, then shared/, then postprocessing/)? Document must pick.\n4. LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion: bundle into the migration, or hold for a separate A/B sprint? Document must pick.\n5. res_2s / self_refiner exposure: off-by-default flags, or promote to default once upstream does? Document must pick.\n6. Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion: does this sit inside the fork migration sprint plan (as a sprint 3 or 4), or is it a separate initiative? Document must pick.\n7. Rebase cadence post-migration: weekly? Monthly? Triggered by specific upstream events? Document must pick.\n8. CI / drift detection: how do we avoid re-drifting once we're synced? Document must propose a specific mechanism (scheduled CI job comparing to upstream head, etc.).\n\nTarget shape of the final document:\n- Executive summary (what, why, how long, what we're protecting)\n- Current-state inventory (what's vendored, what's drifted, what's load-bearing)\n- Fork architecture decision (the Settled Decisions on shape, patch triage, cadence)\n- Sprint breakdown: 3-5 sprints of 2 weeks each, each with: goals, deliverables, verification gates, rollback plan, explicit functionality-preservation checks. First sprint should be inventory-heavy (the diff triage is where the risk lives). Last sprint should cover sustainability (rebase cadence, CI, detection).\n- Risk register: what could go wrong per sprint, probability, impact, mitigation\n- Success criteria: objective checks for migration complete vs migration in progress vs regression observed\n- Explicit Settled Decisions section using the canonical megaplan format (id: SD-NNN, load_bearing, decision, rationale)\n\nOut of scope (document should note these explicitly as non-goals): flipping ltx_anchor to default, real-GPU empirical validation of the new defaults, upscaler-pass anchor re-injection, identity-drift fixes.\n\nHonor the memory-note constraint: do NOT include hard-halt prerequisite clauses of the form halt when MX not shipped. Each sprint must be able to proceed, revise scope, or explicitly deprioritize \u2014 but not sit in force-proceed limbo.",

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

> AGENT

The critique's claims check out — the existing test corpus doesn't cover the cameraman override branch, and importing `Wan2GP.shared.utils.self_refiner` triggers SciPy imports via `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py`. Neither flag signals a wrong root cause; both are surgical fixes to the verification commands. The right answer is: Sprint 3 must *write new tests* for the uncovered override/dedup branches (justified by the brief's "must have a verification step"), and Sprint 2's path smoke test must use filesystem/`sys.path` checks that don't execute `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py`.
